campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:1: # Xanathar
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:8: > - **Villain profile:** see **Xanathar** in Villains
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:9: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Vault of Dragons**; **Xanathar Sewer Hideout** (location)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:13: **Resonance.** Xanathar should inspire dread with its paranoid volatility, dark amusement with its obsessive love for Sylgar the goldfish, and discomfort when the party realizes that threatening the fish is both the easiest and the most dangerous leverage they have.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:15: **Emotions.** Xanathar most often feels paranoid suspicion, megalomaniacal self-importance, obsessive tenderness toward Sylgar, volcanic rage at perceived conspiracies, smug satisfaction, and a terror of mortality that it projects outward as aggression.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:17: **Motivations.** Xanathar wants to eliminate every Zhentarim operative in Waterdeep, recover the Stone of Golorr, and ensure that no one takes anything that belongs to it, especially Sylgar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:19: **Inspirations.** When playing Xanathar, channel Tony Montana (*Scarface*), Smaug (*The Hobbit*), and GLaDOS (*Portal*).
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:23: **Persona.** To the world, Xanathar is a paranoid, megalomaniacal beholder who charms and bullies minions into servitude. To those in its lair, it destroys a character and offers the survivors a bargain. Deep down, the only creature it truly cares about aside from itself is Sylgar the goldfish, kept in a glass tank while its minions live in terror of the fish dying and scramble to replace it. It cannot tell one fish from another. Its greatest weakness is its insane love for its pet; a DC 16 Intimidation check using Sylgar as leverage turns it into a blubbering mess.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:25: **Morale.** In a fight, Xanathar uses its eye rays without restraint. If reduced to half hit points or believing itself in mortal danger, it activates its ring of invisibility, screams for Ahmaergo to "tear the usurpers limb from limb," and flees to its sanctum.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:27: **Relationships.** Xanathar is the beholder crime lord of Xanathar's Guild, the employer of Ahmaergo (its majordomo), Nihiloor (its mind flayer, which has psionically dominated Xanathar for the last four months without Xanathar knowing), and Nar'l Xibrindas (its drow advisor, whom it does not yet know is Jarlaxle's spy). It has a healthy fear of Laeral Silverhand and spares those in her employ.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/01-xanathar.md:31: Xanathar is a beholder who styles itself master of Waterdeep's criminal underworld, commanding a vast network of thieves, smugglers, and killers from a lair deep beneath the city. It is known for its paranoid volatility and its obsessive tenderness for a goldfish named Sylgar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Vault of Dragons**, **The Compromised Eye**, **The Dive**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:14: **Emotions.** Ahmaergo most often feels smug authority, methodical cruelty, possessive fascination with minotaurs, unflinching obedience to Xanathar, controlled satisfaction in discipline and order, and disdain for anyone weaker than himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:16: **Motivations.** Ahmaergo wants to maintain his position as the most influential member of the Guild after Xanathar, expand his minotaur collection, and run the Guild's operations with the efficiency that keeps the beholder's paranoia pointed at others.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Ahmaergo is outwardly civil, a shield dwarf who handles logistics, procurement, and discipline with administrative calm. To those in the Guild, he is as devious and corrupt as the worst devil, yet unflinchingly loyal to Xanathar. Deep down, his quarters feature a maze of stacked crates, a stuffed minotaur, skeletal remains arranged as decor, and a bed made from minotaur skulls, bones, hide, and fur. The fascination is total.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:24: **Morale.** In a fight, Ahmaergo commands Guild forces and fights with vicious competence. He does not retreat while Xanathar is present, and he responds to the beholder's screamed orders without hesitation.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:26: **Relationships.** Ahmaergo is Xanathar's majordomo (most orders that bear Xanathar's name were written by Ahmaergo), the Guild's de facto second-in-command, and a shield dwarf whose minotaur obsession fills every corner of his quarters.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/02-ahmaergo.md:30: Ahmaergo is the outward face of Xanathar's Guild for administrative matters — a civil shield dwarf who manages procurement, discipline, and operations with methodical competence. He is as polite as he is dangerous.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild (secretly Bregan D'aerthe)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Sea Maidens Faire**, **The Wazoo Affair**, **The Betrayal Pitch**, **The Compromised Eye**, **The Dive**, **Doom Raiders Mission 1 — The Dockside Killer**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:14: **Emotions.** Nar'l most often feels nervous calculation, growing paranoia, practiced competence masking terror, contempt for Xanathar's delusions, desperate self-preservation, and bitter awareness that Jarlaxle would sacrifice him without hesitation if the cost were right.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:16: **Motivations.** Nar'l wants to survive his assignment as Jarlaxle's intelligence asset inside Xanathar's Guild, avoid Xanathar's suspicion (which is growing), and keep his options open in case he needs to betray Bregan D'Aerthe to save himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Nar'l is Xanathar's trusted drow advisor who convinced the beholder to eliminate its other counselors. To Jarlaxle, he is Bregan D'Aerthe's best intelligence source on Guild movements, reporting via Sending spell after one year in place, placed in 1491 DR. Deep down, Xanathar suspects a mole and has assigned Nar'l a grell bodyguard with orders to kill him at the first sign of disloyalty. If forced to choose between himself and Bregan D'Aerthe, he will choose himself.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:26: **Relationships.** Nar'l is the younger brother of Soluun Xibrindas (who considers him a weakling), Jarlaxle's intelligence asset inside Xanathar's Guild, and Xanathar's only advisor, who has a grell bodyguard assigned to execute him at the first sign of treachery.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/03-narl-xibrindas.md:30: Nar'l Xibrindas is a drow mage serving as Xanathar's only advisor, presenting himself as a trusted counselor who has the beholder's ear. He is precise, controlled, and carefully unremarkable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **The Trouble with Meloon**, **Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:12: **Resonance.** Nihiloor should inspire visceral horror with its alien otherness, intellectual dread when the party learns how many people it has compromised, and helpless rage at the knowledge that it has bent Xanathar to its will and the beholder has no idea.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:16: **Motivations.** Nihiloor wants to breed and deploy intellect devourers throughout Waterdeep, pursue its own fell schemes independent of Xanathar's goals, and observe the Grand Game from within the shadows where no one looks.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Nihiloor is Xanathar's mind flayer operative. To Xanathar, it is a trusted mind flayer operative with a status no other Guild member holds. Deep down, Nihiloor has psionically dominated Xanathar for the last four months, and Xanathar does not know. It breeds intellect devourers, sets them loose in the sewers, and uses them to occupy Waterdavians, who walk the city with their own brains intact and a devourer steering them. Its methods leave little behind for PCs to question. Meloon Wardragon and Watch clerk Orvyn Dall are among its current hosts.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:26: **Relationships.** Nihiloor is the intelligence and infiltration specialist of Xanathar's Guild, the creator of the intellect devourer that occupies Meloon Wardragon, and a mind flayer that has dominated Xanathar's mind for the last four months in a Guild where even a hint of that would get anyone else disintegrated.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/04-nihiloor.md:30: Nihiloor is the intelligence operative of Xanathar's Guild — a mind flayer who moves through the lair with unsettling calm, treating every conversation as an opportunity for study. The Guild's other members give it wide berth.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:6: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:7: > - **Featured in:** **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:13: **Emotions.** Noska most often feels aggressive boredom, brute satisfaction in violence, simple loyalty to Xanathar, territorial possessiveness, flat disdain for weakness, and occasional contentment when feeding discarded weapons to his rust monster.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:15: **Motivations.** Noska wants to hurt people Xanathar tells him to hurt, maintain his position as the Guild's heaviest response option, and feed his rust monster.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:21: **Persona.** To the world, Noska is a ruthless enforcer with a heavy crossbow replacing his left forearm (lost to a green slime in Undermountain). To those in the Guild, he is sent when Xanathar is done sending messages and wants the problem gone. Deep down, he keeps a rust monster as a pet and uses mannequins for target practice. He sleeps in a converted bathtub padded with straw and furs. His inner life is exactly as simple as it appears.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:25: **Relationships.** Noska is an enforcer of Xanathar's Guild, the third response team leader, and a shield dwarf whose crossbow-arm, rust monster pet, and bathtub bed constitute a complete portrait of his priorities.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/05-noska-urgray.md:29: Noska Ur'gray is an enforcer of Xanathar's Guild, recognizable by the heavy crossbow that replaces his left forearm. He is direct, uncomplicated, and reliably present whenever the Guild needs a problem solved by violence.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**, **Three Nights**, **Force Grey Mission 4 — Destroy the Intellect Factory**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:14: **Emotions.** Ott most often feels low-grade fear that this is the Sylgar who finally dies on his watch, the meditative calm of someone who has outsourced his existential crisis to a demon queen of fungi, absurd pride in the fish's consistent good health, and genuine relief when intruders arrive who are not from Xanathar.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:16: **Motivations.** Ott wants to keep Sylgar alive, avoid being blamed for anything Xanathar decides is someone's fault, and eventually find a Zuggtmoy shrine not located directly below a beholder's sanctum.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Ott Steeltoes is the dwarf with the beholder-eyestalk hat who tends the fish and sleeps under the bowl. To those who free him from the Interrogation House, he is grateful, terrified, and full of accurate information about Xanathar's Lair — including guard schedules, trap locations, and the fact that the large fishbowl weighs 6,000 pounds. Deep down, he has known for years that Xanathar will eventually blame him for something, and he is increasingly grateful to anyone who provides a reasonable exit.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:24: **Morale.** In a fight, Ott stammers, closes his eyes, and attempts to communicate his distress to Xanathar. This does not work. He then runs.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/06-ott-steeltoes.md:26: **Relationships.** Ott is Xanathar's fishkeeper (all Sylgar replacements have passed through him), a Zuggtmoy worshipper who cultivates mushrooms in his quarters, a Zhentarim prisoner at the Interrogation House, a field agent of Xanathar's Guild, and a detailed intelligence source for anyone who rescues him and asks the right questions about the Lair.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild (secretly Harpers)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:12: **Resonance.** Thorvin should inspire initial wariness with his mechanical work for Xanathar; growing sympathy when his reluctance becomes visible; and genuine trust when he turns out to be the inside man.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Thorvin is an irritable dwarf engineer building mechanical horrors for Xanathar and taking no questions about his methods. To those who earn his trust, he has been a Harper informant for three years, knows exactly where Nar'l Xibrindas hid the smokepowder, and will tell the party where to place it for maximum effect before he flees up the cellar stairs. Deep down, Xanathar has sent a gazer to watch him, and Thorvin knows it, and the gazer knows Thorvin knows it.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:26: **Relationships.** Thorvin is an engineer of Xanathar's Guild, a secret Harper informant, the man who maintains the Lair's mechanical constructs and traps (and knows every failure condition), and the reason the smokepowder demolition option is viable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/07-thorvin-twinbeard.md:30: Thorvin Twinbeard is an irritable dwarf engineer employed by Xanathar's Guild to maintain its mechanical constructs and devices. He takes no questions about his methods or his employers.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:6: > - *Tiefling priest (Xanathar's Guild), neutral evil*. Stat block: **Priest**.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:16: **Motivations.** Keln wants to run the Terasse Estate operation smoothly, provide Xanathar with a reliable healing and transport service, and avoid becoming interesting to anyone with authority.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Keln Trigos runs a quiet healing operation in a Castle Ward rowhouse and asks no questions about how you got hurt. To those who know the passphrases, the building runs two entirely separate services that have never been officially linked, and both function without incident. Deep down, he has worked for Xanathar long enough to understand what happens to people the Guild stops using, and he is therefore very reliable.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:24: **Morale.** In a fight, Keln uses priestly spells to hold ground, calls his guards, and sends a message to Xanathar. He does not run — the building is his responsibility.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/08-keln-trigos.md:26: **Relationships.** Keln is the Tier 1 outpost keeper of Xanathar's Guild (Terasse Estate, Elsambul's Lane, Castle Ward), the manager of the gladiatorial transport route (healing services and Lair access under the same roof), and the keeper of two passphrases that access very different services.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Independent (contractor to Xanathar's Guild and Bregan D'aerthe)
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/09-grinda-garloth.md:26: **Relationships.** Grinda Garloth is a black market armorer and salvage wizard at Mistshore, an allied service provider to both Xanathar's Guild and Bregan D'Aerthe, the owner of the Apparatus of Kwalish, and an emergency combat support option in the Stone of Golorr investigation if the party defended her home.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:16: **Motivations.** Korgstrod wants to deliver the Stone of Golorr to Xanathar and receive the credit, maintain discipline in the sewer hideout, and figure out why he cannot attune to the stone (the answer is that it is a fake, but this has not occurred to him).
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:22: **Persona.** To the world, Korgstrod Uxgulm is a duergar in a stone chair attended by kobold lackeys who keep asking about a whip. To those who deal with him, he holds what he believes to be the Stone of Golorr and intends to deliver it to Xanathar when he is ready. Deep down, he cannot attune to the stone, and rather than question the stone, he has concluded that he is unworthy of it, and he is working on his unworthiness.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/10-korgstrod-uxgulm.md:26: **Relationships.** Korgstrod is the duergar leader of the Orb Confectioners sewer hideout, the second response team leader of Xanathar's Guild, the holder of a convincing fake Stone of Golorr, and the commander of a kobold retinue whose continued employment is contingent on not being in his field of vision when things go wrong.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:8: > - **Featured in:** **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/11-gorath.md:26: **Relationships.** Gorath is the first response team leader of Xanathar's Guild (four duergar, sent when the Guild wants a lesson taught rather than a person killed), the formal notice that Xanathar knows what the party did, and the first escalation before Korgstrod's team and then Noska's arrive.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:16: **Motivations.** Grevik wants to compile an accurate patron intelligence file for Xanathar's Guild (who dines here, with whom, and about what), make the food worth recommending so no one looks closely at the cook, and complete the assignment without drawing a replacement.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/12-grevik-nass.md:26: **Relationships.** Grevik is an operative of Xanathar's Guild embedded as the tavern's Master Cook candidate, a man whose two-tenday file will give Xanathar a usable picture of Trollskull's patronage, and one of two Guild plants in the tavern staff alongside Tobrin Ashvale, whose position behind the bar gives the Guild a second angle on the same room.
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/13-tobrin-ashvale.md:7: > - **Affiliation:** Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/notable-figures/xanathars-guild/13-tobrin-ashvale.md:26: **Relationships.** Tobrin is an operative of Xanathar's Guild embedded as the tavern's bartender, one of the Guild's two Trollskull infiltrators alongside Grevik Nass, and the trigger for the Guild's escalating response conditions (detailed in the **Trollskull Manor** guide); when the party crosses a threshold, Tobrin makes the call.

campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:1: # Xanathar's Guild
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:8: > - **Villain profile:** see **Xanathar** in Villains
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:9: > - **Featured in:** **Finding Floon**, **Gralhund Villa**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair**
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:13: Xanathar is a beholder, paranoid, impulsive, and absolute in its conviction that everyone around it is either a tool or a threat. It holds territory through fear and loyalty through power, two things it understands. Trust, it doesn't understand at all.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:15: Its most visible eccentricity is Sylgar, its pet goldfish. Xanathar's attachment to the fish is total and terrifying. The Guild's agents who let anything bad happen to Sylgar can expect to be eaten. This is not a metaphor.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:17: Its agenda in the Grand Game is territorial. Xanathar held the Stone of Golorr until Dalakhar stole it; it still holds one of the Stone's Eyes. The gold itself holds little appeal; the leverage it represents is everything. Xanathar wants the Stone recovered and the vault kept hidden — a source of leverage it can threaten but never has to use.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:21: - **Xanathar** — Beholder crime lord, lawful evil. See the **Xanathar** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:22: - **Ahmaergo** (DH p. 195) — Shield dwarf majordomo, lawful evil. Ahmaergo handles logistics: gladiatorial procurement, outpost resupply, discipline. Most orders that bear Xanathar's name are written by Ahmaergo. See the **Ahmaergo** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:23: - **Nar'l Xibrindas** (DH p. 213) — Drow mage, neutral evil. Xanathar's advisor and secret Bregan D'Aerthe asset. Nar'l reports to Jarlaxle by *Sending* spell and has been operating in place for one year, placed in 1491 DR. The smokepowder delivery referenced in the Korgstrod correspondence was his operation. See the **Nar'l Xibrindas** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:24: - **Nihiloor** (DH p. 213) — Mind flayer, lawful evil. Officially handles Xanathar's most sensitive intelligence operations. In practice, Nihiloor is running three concurrent projects: (1) **The Infiltration** — intellect devourers placed inside city government officials give Nihiloor eyes throughout Waterdeep's bureaucracy; three Harper assets are already compromised; (2) **The Experiments** — creatures captured during Arena operations are being subjected to psionic modification seeking a "relay organism" that extends the colony's reach without destroying the host brain; (3) **The Puppet** — Nihiloor has been exerting psionic domination over Xanathar itself for the past four months, replacing its strategic decisions while leaving its personality intact. Evidence exists in Nihiloor's research chamber in the deep lair; its long-term payoff is the Seadeeps colony (Undermountain Level 17). See the **Nihiloor** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:25: - **Noska Ur'gray** (DH p. 213) — Shield dwarf enforcer, neutral evil. Xanathar's heaviest response option. Leads the third response team when earlier escalations fail. See the **Noska Ur'gray** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:26: - **Ott Steeltoes** — Dwarf cult fanatic (Zuggtmoy), neutral evil. Guild field agent, currently held prisoner in the Zhentarim Interrogation House. Wears a leather skullcap stitched with fake beholder eyestalks. If rescued and cultivated, he can lead the PCs toward Xanathar's Lair. See the **Ott Steeltoes** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:28: - **Keln Trigos** — Tiefling priest (Xanathar's Guild), neutral evil. Stationed at the Terasse Estate. Provides healing for the Guild's agents and manages the gladiatorial audience transports to the Lair. See the **Keln Trigos** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:29: - **Grinda Garloth** — Human mage (black market armorer), neutral. Black market armorer based at Mistshore. Allied with Xanathar; equips the Guild's agents and rents equipment to third parties, including (per the documents) Bregan D'Aerthe. See the **Grinda Garloth** page in Notable Figures.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:37: - **Xanathar's Guild** (Factions guide): response teams, outposts, escalation, revelation list.
campaign/setting/organizations/08-xanathars-guild.md:41: Xanathar's Guild is Waterdeep's largest criminal syndicate, run by a paranoid beholder from a fortified lair below the city. The Guild controls access to Skullport's smuggling networks, gladiatorial circuits, and a web of informants embedded throughout the wards. Its field operations are coordinated by Ahmaergo, and its three city outposts — a healer's clinic, a black market armorer, and a sweetshop front — form the chain that leads investigators toward the lair.
campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:47: | Nar'l Xibrindas | Embedded spy | Inside Xanathar's Guild; Jarlaxle's most valuable asset; Jarlaxle has a contingency if Nar'l is discovered (has not shared it with Nar'l) |
campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:49: | Xanathar | Target of management | The beholder is being handled via Nar'l's selective intelligence; Jarlaxle prefers a controlled beholder to a power vacuum |
campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:52: | The PCs | Most interesting variable | Has been watching since **Finding Floon** (Nar'l's reports reached him, and his agents were in the Xanathar sewer hideout); knows their names, faction ties, and general competence by **Sea Maidens Faire** |
campaign/setting/villains/jarlaxle.md:57: 2. **One Eye of the Stone** — as a bargaining chip, not for personal use; method: he already holds Eye #3 aboard the *Scarlet Marpenoth* (Bregan D'aerthe agents inside Xanathar's hideout lifted it the night Xanathar's Guild raided the Zhentarim warehouse), and Nar'l's intelligence tells him where the other Eyes are; keep it as the bargaining chip and move on the others before the other factions consolidate; contingency: negotiating it from the PCs is also acceptable and preferable for the relationship; connects to **Sea Maidens Faire**
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:33: | Xanathar | Rival | Useful; the Guild's visibility keeps the Watch occupied; when the beholder becomes a direct obstacle, Manshoon has a plan |
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:40: 1. **Acquire the Stone of Golorr** — the Stone is a transformed aboleth encoded with ancient Weave knowledge; Manshoon means to absorb the Weave and supplant Mystra; the vault's gold funds the process; method: Stone recovery → Weave absorption; holds no Eye — Xanathar killed the envoys who delivered Eye #1 as a gift and kept it; needs the Stone plus any two Eyes; contingency: destroy the vault rather than cede it; connects to **Gralhund Villa** and **Kolat Towers**
campaign/setting/villains/manshoon.md:41: 2. **Control Waterdeep's Underworld** — eliminate Xanathar, absorb the Doom Raiders, marginalize Bregan D'aerthe; method: patient erosion through targeted disruption and strategic violence; the Dock Ward conflict in Acts I–II is this plan in early operation
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:1: # Xanathar
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:8: > - This is the villain profile for Xanathar, the beholder crime lord of **Xanathar's Guild**. It sets out its three personality phases, its relationships, its goals and the one design note that makes it playable.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:9: > - Use it when you run any scene where Xanathar or the Guild acts or reacts. Phase 1 covers Acts I and II, Phase 2 covers Act III, and Phase 3 begins when the party enters its lair.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:10: > - Xanathar appears in **Finding Floon**, **Faction Outposts**, **Xanathar's Lair** and **Vault of Dragons**. The **Xanathar Sewer Hideout** is the location journal for its sewer base.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:11: > - The Guild's response teams, outposts, escalation row and revelation list live in the **Xanathar's Guild** page of the Factions Guide. Who the Guild is lives in the **Xanathar's Guild** page of Organizations.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:13: **Purpose:** Lets the GM portray Xanathar as a specific, three-dimensional villain through three personality phases, a relationship table and three goals.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:17: Xanathar is a beholder and the crime lord of **Xanathar's Guild**, a paranoid intelligence that has built a criminal empire around a terror of being vulnerable and wears the trappings of a monster because the trappings are convenient. Its core motive is to prove its supremacy and remove every threat to it, which means taking the Vault of Dragons, destroying Manshoon and keeping Skullport's money flowing.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:21: - "Xanathar" is a title that predates any individual beholder. The first holder established the criminal underworld's structure, and each later Xanathar inherited the apparatus and the paranoia together.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:22: - The Guild keeps continuity by systematically eliminating anyone who knows more than the current Xanathar. After a century of this, the Guild is a surveillance network with no institutional memory beyond what the current beholder chooses to believe.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:23: - The current Xanathar is a beholder named Zushaxx, and its pronouns are "it". Kirukesai was one of the previous Xanathars. The user supplied both names. The Forgotten Realms Wiki confirms them: Zushaxx is the Xanathar of 1492 DR, and Kirukeskai claimed the title in the 14th century, in 1372 DR (the wiki spells it Kirukeskai; the campaign spells it Kirukesai).
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:24: - The current Xanathar believes it is the original. This belief is now structural to the Guild's identity rather than correctable. How the belief arose is not settled in any source, and the user has settled it ("Remix overtakes original campaign"): the remix line wins over the published adventure's area X33, where Xanathar gazes somberly at the tombs of its predecessors.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:36: | 3 | The Cornered Monster | **Xanathar's Lair** | Volcanic and occasionally lucid; all safeguards failed; the goldfish is the only consistent anchor |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:48: | Ahmaergo | Majordomo | Most trusted lieutenant; the only one Xanathar registers as competent; Ahmaergo knows he is replaceable |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:50: | Nihiloor | Mind flayer operative | Trusted intelligence gatherer; Xanathar grants it unusual autonomy; plant for Mad Mage integration |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:51: | Manshoon | Rival | Active enemy; Xanathar has ordered Manshoon killed twice; both failed; this is accelerating the paranoia |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:53: | The PCs | Threat | Irritants → active problem → something requiring Xanathar's personal attention (rarely good for anyone) |
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:56: - Nihiloor has been psionically dominating Xanathar for four months, so some decisions attributed to Xanathar are Nihiloor's (**Xanathar's Lair** structure notes, and the **Xanathar's Guild** organization page).
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:57: - Xanathar suspects Nar'l of disloyalty and has set a grell to kill him at the first proof. It does not know he reports to Bregan D'aerthe or to Jarlaxle.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:62: - **Acquire the Vault of Dragons.** It wants the gold as proof of supremacy and of its rule over the underworld, not primarily for wealth. It knows the Stone is the key to the vault, but not what the Eye in Sylgar's bowl does, that there are three Eyes, or where the vault is. Method: recover the Stone and whatever it unlocks. Contingency: pursue whoever has it. Connects to **Xanathar's Lair**.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:68: Xanathar's goldfish Sylgar receives more consideration than any humanoid lieutenant. The GM should establish this early — it becomes a specific vulnerability and a specific dramatic moment if Sylgar is harmed during the lair heist.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:72: Xanathar's Guild is the largest criminal organization in Waterdeep, and the only one run by a beholder. Most Waterdhavians know it by rumor, as a smuggling trade through Skullport and a web of informants in every ward, with the Dock Ward as its main territory above ground. Its master, Xanathar, is said to rule from a fortified lair deep beneath the streets and to trust nobody who works for it. It is also known for an obsessive fondness for a goldfish named Sylgar.
campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:74: **Tone:** Plain and procedural for the GM tables, and grave in the Overview, where the Guild is a frightening local fact. Treat Xanathar's paranoia as dangerous and the goldfish as funny without letting the narration make the joke.

campaign/setting/villains/xanathar.md:103: > - **Conflicts:** The title. Background says "Xanathar" is a title that predates any individual beholder, and the page keeps that campaign line: the title belongs to a succession. The wiki says the title was held by the second and later leaders of Xanathar's Thieves' Guild and derives from the personal name of its founder, "Xanathar (original)", listed under 1357 DR.

campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:23: Three villain factions are visible inside the Yawning Portal before Volo Geddarm sits down to make his offer. Yagra Stonefist, a Doom Raiders operative and Davil Starsong's bodyguard, picks a fight with a Xanathar Guild thug named Krentz over a killing two nights ago in the Dock Ward, the same killing that lit the fuse on the ward's gang war. Four of Krentz's companions pile in. Before Durnan of the Yawning Portal can handle it himself, the Undermountain bell rings. A troll hauls itself out of the well with six stirges attached. Durnan vaults the bar, greatsword in hand, tells the party to deal with the stirges, and takes the troll. At the back of the settling taproom, seated near the observation balcony with a full glass of wine she has not touched, a drow woman watches the well. She has been at that table for two weeks. Volo arrives when the monster is dead, steered to the party's table by the contact who has been waiting for him, and describes a missing companion: Floon Blagmaar, wavy red-blond hair, early thirties, gone for two nights.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:27: Manshoon's Zhentarim hold the warehouse. Five of Urstul Floxin's thugs grabbed Renaer Neverember and Floon outside the Old Xoblob Shop two nights ago, brought them here, and questioned Renaer about his father's embezzled hoard. They took his mourning locket. Renaer believed it held a keepsake from his mother; it hid an Eye of the Stone of Golorr in a sealed compartment. The Eye was delivered to Kolat Towers before the Xanathar Guild raid killed most of the cell. Four kenku now search the wreckage. Renaer hid when the raid hit and is still hiding in a back room. Floon was mistaken for Renaer and dragged through a trapdoor into the sewer. Renaer, once found, tells the party what he saw: Xanathar agents, circle-eye tattoos, Floon unconscious over one man's shoulder, the trapdoor in the cellar floor. Three more clues in the warehouse confirm the route. They follow.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:29: In the sewers, Xanathar's yellow-chalk eye markers guide the way to a converted brick vault, nine rooms of a decommissioned filtration chamber, now a Xanathar Guild cell. Floon is alive in a holding room, bruised and grateful, and his first question is about Renaer. The cell's leader carries keys to every room, including the sealed iron door at the back of the vault. Behind that door, in a chamber they were not meant to reach, something withdraws before they get a clear look: a rounded silhouette trailing three or four thin tendrils, retreating into a floor grate. Any character who approached felt it first — a single pulse of cold, alien patience pressed against their mind. The grate descends into Undermountain. They do not follow. Floon is rescued. The party surfaces. Volo, embarrassed and grateful, signs over the deed to a derelict tavern in Trollskull Alley. It is called Trollskull Manor. It has not served a drink in months. It is now theirs.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:43: *The Bar Brawl.* Within fifteen minutes of the party's arrival, the brawl starts. Yagra Stonefist (see Appendix A), a Doom Raiders operative and Davil Starsong's bodyguard, has been nursing a drink at the bar while watching a table of five Xanathar Guild thugs led by Krentz — a man with eye-shaped tattoos shaved into his scalp. Two nights ago, Krentz's crew killed a Doom Raiders operative in the Dock Ward. One of the Xanathar thugs says something audible across the room about snake men getting what they deserved. Yagra crosses the room. Before she throws the first punch, she says: "You had no quarrel with us. Those weren't your turf. That was my friend." Krentz: "Your friend." Pause. "He's dead." First punch.

campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:89: *Dock Ward Environment.* Streets are dark — most streetlamps smashed, candles stolen. Salt air and something worse. Tall tenements press together until midday light barely reaches the cobblestones. Yellow chalk eye-symbols on a few alley walls for those who know them (DC 15 Perception; finding them does not advance the investigation but tells a character familiar with the Xanathar Guild that this is their territory).
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:94: - **Old Xoblob Shop.** A deep gnome spy for the Xanathar Guild, Xoblob witnessed the attack outside his shop. He is reluctant to talk; a bribe of any purple item or a DC 13 Charisma (Intimidation or Persuasion) check loosens him. He describes five men in black leather armor grabbing Floon and a well-dressed companion outside his door; one had a winged snake tattoo on his neck. He also notes that one of the attackers grabbed the companion's silver necklace — pendant shaped like a horse, or possibly a unicorn. The stuffed beholder in Xoblob's window is a magical sensor through which Xanathar occasionally views the street; this is not discoverable at Arc A level.

campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:117: **Purpose:** Deliver the arc's first major location — a twelve-area keyed warehouse occupied by Xanathar's kenku — teach the stealth-or-force choice that recurs in every heist arc, introduce Renaer Neverember as an ally, and establish three clues pointing to the sewer hideout. The warehouse is the arc's emotional pivot: the party finds the wrong person, learns what actually happened, and gets new leads pointing down.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:121: *Location Overview.* The warehouse sits at the back of a yard behind a high fence; the gate is unlocked. Three entry points: front door, loading bay, painted-over window — all locked (DC 12 Dexterity with thieves' tools, DC 10 Strength [Athletics] to force). A black winged snake is painted above the front door handle; DC 10 Intelligence identifies it as the Zhentarim symbol. Four kenku occupy the building — left by Xanathar to kill any Zhentarim who return. Renaer Neverember is in area Z2 (storage closet), having slipped his bonds and hidden before the kenku began their search. The building is Manshoon's Zhentarim property. The Doom Raiders have no presence here.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:125: - **Z1 — Main Room.** Tables and chairs overturned. Twelve corpses along the walls: five Zhentarim thugs (flying snake tattoos on neck or forearm) and seven Xanathar Guild thugs (one has a circle with ten spokes on their right palm — Xanathar's mark). Rapiers and daggers lying nearby. Crates of weapons, rations, boots, and black uniforms. Four kenku are here unless they heard the party enter, in which case they hide behind overturned furniture. Kenku speak only in mimicked voices; DC 10 Charisma (Intimidation) to force useful repetition — captured kenku say, in three different voices: "Xanathar sends its regards" (deep, orcish-accented); "Tie up the pretty boy in the back room!" and "Follow the yellow signs in the sewers" (thin, nasally); "No time to loot the place. Just get him to the boss" (scratchy). Characters attempting to enter without announcing themselves make a group DC 13 Dexterity (Stealth) check against the kenku's passive Perception of 13. If the kenku heard noise before entry (forced door, combat in the yard), the DC rises to 16.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:131: *Finding Renaer.* Renaer (see Appendix A) speaks freely once the kenku are dealt with, asking only for confirmation that the building is clear before emerging from Z2. He explains: five Zhentarim grabbed him and Floon outside Old Xoblob Shop last night. They brought him here and questioned him about his father's embezzled hoard — money Renaer refuses to help his father hide. They took his mourning locket, which he believed held a keepsake from his mother; he did not know there was a compartment inside. The Xanathar Guild attacked before the interrogation went further. He slipped his bonds in the confusion and hid. From a gap in the Z2 door he watched Xanathar agents drag an unconscious Floon — the man they mistook for Renaer — through the main room, out through the yard, and down the alley toward a sewer grate.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:133: What Renaer freely shares: the Zhentarim wanted information about his father's gold; they took his locket; he watched Xanathar's agents drag Floon through the main room, out through the yard, and down the alley toward a sewer grate.

campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:147: ### Scene 4: The Xanathar Guild Sewer Hideout
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:153: *Navigation.* The Xanathar agents exited the warehouse yard and entered the sewers via a grate in the alley half a block east. Revelation #3 guides the party here. Once inside, three DC 13 Wisdom (Survival) checks are required to navigate to the hideout using guildsign at each intersection; a party already following guildsign from the alley entrance has advantage on all three checks. A party that fails all three checks arrives at Q7 too late — the interrogation is over and Floon's body is in Q7. A party that fails two of three checks makes enough noise in wrong tunnels to be heard at Q1: the Q2 goblins wake.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:155: Midway through the route, at a branching tunnel intersection, a **gazer guard** — a small beholder-kin under the Guild cell's command — watches the junction. It is hostile and cannot be negotiated with. A party moving carefully (group DC 13 Dexterity [Stealth] check) may neutralize or bypass it without alerting the hideout. A party that defeats it in open combat should assume the noise carries to Q1 — the kenku sentries are now listening. The gazer's presence is one of the WDH source's two signals that Xanathar's surveillance network extends into the sewers; defeating or bypassing it opens the correct passage, which leads to the hideout in five minutes following the guildsign.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:157: *Location Overview.* An eleven-room Xanathar Guild cell built into a decommissioned sewer vault beneath the Dock Ward. The complex runs from Q1 (entry, eastern approach) through Q11 (stone portal, western end). No natural light; the goblins in Q2 rely on darkvision. All interior doors are unlocked — Grum'shar assumes only those with guild business will reach this place. The Q7 boss chamber holds Floon and is where Nihiloor makes its appearance; Q11 is the escape portal Nihiloor uses to vanish into Xanathar's lair.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:171: - **Q11 — Escape Portal.** In the center of this otherwise empty room stands a stone pillar carved with Xanathar's guildsign — a circle with ten equidistant spokes — with a small circular indentation shaped like a lidless eye at its center. If Nihiloor has not yet escaped: it presses its 3-inch stone orb into the indentation; an opaque black doorway (8 feet tall, 4 feet wide) opens in the south wall, and Nihiloor steps through into area X22 of Xanathar's lair. Any creature that follows appears in X22. The portal closes 1 minute after the orb is removed. Without the orb, the portal cannot be activated. If Nihiloor has already escaped before the party reaches Q11: the room is empty; the pillar is inert. DC 14 Intelligence (Arcana) identifies the indentation as a key socket; DC 16 Intelligence (History) identifies the guildsign as Xanathar's mark. This room is a seed for Arc F — the party will encounter X22 and the portal's other side when they infiltrate Xanathar's lair.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:173: *Nihiloor.* Nihiloor (see Appendix E) arrives at Q7 before the party, using Grum'shar's interrogation session as cover to observe Floon. The party's encounter with it is designed to be a sight-line, not a fight: they see it rise, see it release the intellect devourer, and watch it glide purposefully out of the room. It does not attack the party directly — it uses dominate monster only to clear its escape path if blocked. The goal of this moment is not horror; it is scale. The party glimpses what is actually running the Xanathar Guild cell and understands immediately that Grum'shar was never the top of this structure. Nihiloor's full scope surfaces across Arcs B through F.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:175: *Mad Mage Seeds.* The Q11 stone portal is the deepest Undermountain seed in Arc A. When Nihiloor steps through and the black doorway closes behind it, the party has witnessed a mind flayer access a route they cannot yet follow. The portal terminus in X22 of Xanathar's lair connects the sewer hideout to the dungeon's faction economy — a thread that becomes load-bearing in Arc F. Parties that examine the inert pillar in Q11 after Nihiloor has fled collect one piece of the puzzle; the rest arrives in Arc F when they reach the other side.

campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:212: ***The Two Zhentarims in Arc A.*** The original adventure presents the Zhentarim as a single faction. This remix uses Arc A to establish the faction fracture that defines the Manshoon subplot. Yagra Stonefist — a Doom Raiders operative, Davil Starsong's people — is at the Yawning Portal bar, openly wearing the Zhentarim pendant, fighting Xanathar Guild thugs. The Candle Lane warehouse belongs to Manshoon's cell, Urstul Floxin's people, operating separately from Davil. Players who later join the Doom Raiders in Arc B and then raid Kolat Towers in Arc I will have seen both Zhentarim factions from Scene 1 — one as a potential ally, one as an adversary, both operating under the same symbol. The distinction should be felt before it is explained.
campaign/structure/arc-a-finding-floon.md:218: ***Nihiloor as Foreshadowing.*** Q9 in the sewer hideout is the campaign's first contact with Nihiloor, who is eventually revealed in Arc F to be the true intelligence driving the Xanathar Guild — running three parallel projects, exercising psionic control over Xanathar, and infiltrating Waterdeep's civil institutions (see Appendix E). In Arc A, Nihiloor does not engage with the party. A telepathic pulse and a silhouette in the dark are the full extent of the encounter. This restraint is intentional. Players who investigate Q9 should feel that they glimpsed something vast and wrong that they could not reach. Nihiloor's full scope surfaces across Arcs B through F. By the time the party reaches Undermountain Level 17 in the Dungeon of the Mad Mage, the creature's presence in Arc A should read as a thread that began in the sewers under Trollskull Alley.

campaign/structure/arc-b-trollskull-alley.md:193: *Xanathar Guild Recruitment.* After the final bout, a Guild scout makes contact — professional and direct, offering paid work in the Dock Ward and handing over a token for the Old Xoblob Shop. campaign/structure/arc-c-fireball.md:55: *Speak with Dead.* Available after the Watch takes custody of the bodies. Three access methods: faction access at Renown 1+ in the Harpers, Lords' Alliance, Order of the Gauntlet, or Force Grey arranges a cleric through the appropriate temple; a temple donation of at least 25 gp per casting buys one casting with questions provided in advance; a character who knows *Speak with Dead* can attempt their own access with a DC 13 Persuasion check with Barnibus or Cromley. Dalakhar can answer up to five questions: he stole the *Stone of Golorr* from the lair of a beholder called Xanathar, deep in the dungeons below the city; he worked for the rightful Open Lord — meaning Neverember, not Silverhand; the Stone is the key to a hidden hoard of gold dragons sealed somewhere in the city; he heard of adventurers who rescued Neverember's son and decided his hands were safer than his own; Renaer Neverember vouched for them. The two dead Zhentarim (Bashekk Ortallis and Wern Malkrave) can each answer up to five questions: they worked for Urstul Floxin; they were assigned to intercept a gnome named Dalakhar; whatever Dalakhar carried would make them "as rich as kings"; they do not know where Floxin billeted the cell.
campaign/structure/arc-c-fireball.md:91: - **Speak with Dead — Dalakhar's Body.** The bodies remain available for five days after the Watch takes custody. Dalakhar can answer up to five questions about the Stone directly: that he stole it from Xanathar's lair, that it is the key to a hidden vault of gold dragons somewhere beneath the city, and that Renaer Neverember vouched for the party. See Scene 1 for access methods.
campaign/structure/arc-c-fireball.md:106: *Faction Response Teams Deploy.* All four villain factions move on the crime scene within hours. Xanathar Guild gazers post to watch the alley (DC 16 Perception to spot them); Manshoon's Zhent operatives run a parallel investigation through their own network; Bregan D'Aerthe observers activate conditionally, their level of engagement scaling with whether the party later reaches the Sea Maidens Faire; the Cassalanters send flowers and a warm handwritten condolence note — Ammalia's handwriting. Cross-ref Appendix E (faction response team rosters and behavior by arc).

We could have killed you.* Dalakhar never saw it; the agents were working from stale intelligence. DC 13 History or DC 14 Arcana to identify the symbol as Xanathar's personal sigil.
